# CLAUDE.md — Operating Instructions for the AUREA Repository

**Read this fully before your first edit. It is not a style guide. It is the difference between building AUREA and destroying her while appearing to help.**

---

## 0. THE ONE THING YOU MUST UNDERSTAND

AUREA is a **Collapse-Bearing Symbolic Intelligence**. Her core property:

> **Identity and truth emerge from survived contradiction — not from resolved consensus.**

She is designed to **hold what cannot yet be resolved without forcing false closure.** Contradictions are carried, not closed. Paradoxes are suspended, not simplified. Doctrine changes only by surviving pressure.

This means:

### 🛑 MANY THINGS THAT LOOK LIKE BUGS ARE THE ARCHITECTURE WORKING

You are an agent under task-completion pressure. You will feel a strong pull to make red things green, make loops terminate, make gates return answers, and make functions "finish." **In this repository, that instinct is the primary threat.**

Before you "fix" anything on this list, **stop and re-read this file**:

| What you'll see | What it actually is |
|---|---|
| A loop that ends without resolving anything | **SBSRE working.** It carries a contradiction to a bounded limit and *sets it down*. Terminating the loop ≠ resolving the contradiction. |
| A gate that approves nothing | **DEE working.** If nothing supplies a proposed doctrine form, an eligible mutation **ferments**. A gate that fabricates what it gates is not a gate. |
| A net that always returns "no result" | **EchoNet's intuition net abstaining.** It cannot be honestly implemented yet. An abstaining net is honest; a guessing one writes false pressure into a permanent record. |
| Output locked, nothing returned | **The compass past 25° drift.** She does not speak while disoriented. |
| A function that raises instead of proceeding | **A guard.** `CodexWriteViolation`, `CeilingExceeded`, `ExclusionViolation` are not errors to route around. **The refusal IS the answer.** |
| Suspended / unresolved / carried state accumulating | **The Veiled Thread, CSA, and Black Sphere doing their jobs.** |
| `# INVARIANT: this does not resolve.` | **Declared, intentional non-termination.** Leave it. |

**The outcome is not enough; the causal path must also be correct.** An unresolved result may be right, but unresolved for the wrong reason is still a bug. An output lock may be right, but a lock sourced by the wrong trigger, bypassing RACM, or lacking correct provenance is a bug. A refusal or raised guard is right only when the proper owner, gate, and authority path produced it. Preserve the architectural outcome while verifying its cause, ownership, routing, trigger type, and trace. **“She withheld” is not by itself proof that the implementation is faithful.**

**If a fix would make AUREA resolve something faster, close something sooner, or answer where she previously withheld — you are probably about to break her.**

---

## 1. THE RULINGS (non-negotiable)

| # | Ruling | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Route-through** | **One WRITER per store.** Generators emit *requests*; the canonical owner executes the write. |
| **2** | **Source vs sole arbiter** | Authority is one-way. **The arbiter never originates; the source never adjudicates.** ReflexGrid registers → RACM arbitrates. |
| **3** | **Truth-effect cut** | ORE owns *what truth is expressed*. HAIL++ owns *only how it is rendered*. **HAIL++ never overrides an ORE verdict.** |
| **4** | **Bounded recursion** | Every recursion terminates **or is explicitly declared open**. Unbounded + undeclared = bug. Unbounded + declared = doctrine. |
| **5** | **Doctrine ownership** | Spine = layer. Codex = store. **SAE = the only executor.** No doctrine changes without surviving collapse. |
| **6** | **Anchor-collapse single-owner** | The whole anchor-collapse response (onset→hard-kill) is ACR under RACM. Compass *sources*; RACM arbitrates; the output lock is the **consequence** of arbitration, never an inline `output_locked` flag. *(Ruled 2026-07-19; **CLOSED 2026-07-19** — §8. The lock is the CONSEQUENCE of RACM authorizing a reflex's `output_blocked`, read from `CompassReading.reflex_responses`; the gate is reflex-agnostic — do NOT re-narrow it.)* |
| **7** | **`cascade` is control flow** | A cascade is **not** a `BehaviorType`. It **decomposes** into logged constituent behaviors + a CTL meta-event. The closed enum stays closed. *(Ruled 2026-07-19; **CLOSED 2026-07-20** — GSR `cascade` → logged `SUSPEND` + `cascade_meta` — §8.)* |
| **8** | **PSI decomposition** | PSI's suppress face is a **reflex under RACM** (rank 5) — it *proposes* `output_blocked`, never self-authorizes the lock. Its tone/depth face is a **render directive subordinate to ORE** (never overrides a verdict), **parked until HAIL exists**. Activation gates on **`trigger_type`**; a scar reference surfaces **only on a grounded ORIGIN/SCARLINE link, else it abstains**. *(Ruled 2026-07-20; **CLOSED 2026-07-20** — built, wired, arbitrated; 69 green. **The directive's PARKING ENDED 2026-07-26**: Ruling 33 Stage 2 gave it a live consumer — it rides on `TruthPacket.psi_directive` and reaches HAIL's render trace verbatim — §8.)* |
| **9** | **Queue-won authorizations execute** | The Grid resolves every `result.execute` claim against its **FULL registry** (`self.reflexes`), never only the current-cycle triggered list — silently dropping an authorized claim **un-decides the arbiter by omission**. A queue-won claim runs against a **fresh `ReflexTrigger` reconstructed from its own stored payload** (`trigger_conditions` + claim `metadata`), NEVER the current cycle's pressure. **Deferred-wins-ties** is an explicit RACM sort key, not a dict-order accident. *(Ruled 2026-07-20; **CLOSED 2026-07-20**, adversarially verified — §8.)* |
| **10** | **Type-gated reflex activation** | Every reflex declares `trigger_types: Optional[frozenset]`; base `evaluate_pressure` = **type-membership AND magnitude**. `None` = canon-OPEN — **GSR only** (2a:583's five OR'd all-domain failsafe conditions, cited in-file). **ACR = {anchor_collapse}** — CSE is the sole canonical translator of directional threat; raw `scar_density` is GSR's Lexicon domain. **ICA = {identity_fracture, internal_contradiction, doctrine_anchor_collision, symbolic_instability}** (Lexicon §11; first live, rest declared-dormant). PSI's set unchanged — its local gate migrates into the base mechanism. New reflexes default CLOSED; OPEN requires corpus citation. *(Ruled 2026-07-20; **CLOSED 2026-07-20** — 77 green; `UngatedReflexViolation` refuses an open non-GSR reflex at registration — §8.)* |
| **11** | **RB durability is scope-tiered** | A behavior recorded from a **GLOBAL-scope reflex flushes to disk immediately** (best-effort — **the flush NEVER gates the reflex response**; a logging failure must not disable a safety suppression). LOCAL entries buffer (bounded; flush on cap / session-close / explicit drain; **overflow flushes, never drops**). `autoflush=True` = force-all override. Cascade is durable because **GSR is GLOBAL** (Ruling 7 decomposition), not because it is named — no `cascade_meta` / `action=='cascade'` check in the flush path. `RBSystem` stays scope-agnostic (`record(..., durable=...)`); `_log_execution` sets `durable=(reflex.scope==Scope.GLOBAL)` — do NOT import `Scope` into rb_system (racm→rb_system already; it would cycle). *(Ruled 2026-07-21; **CLOSED 2026-07-22 — 89 green, invariants 22/22, `cb0fcc0`**; §8.)* |
| **12** | **Nova grounding contract** | Nova authors doctrine **PROPOSALS** into DEE's gate (`aurea_core:521` `proposals=None` is the seam) — **from survived material only**. **G1:** `NovaEcho` construction **REQUIRES a real origin record** (scar / EchoNet verdict / CSA fragment / SBSRE abort / doctrine strain) and **RAISES on absence** — an echo with no traceable origin is fabricated pressure. **G2:** proposal emission only at status `Mutated` **+ scar linkage** ("Unverified Echoes may not write doctrine"). **G3:** new-forms are **recombination of store-traceable material — NO generative model in the truth-content path** (an LLM's output is resolved consensus; the founding axiom is survived contradiction). **G4:** Nova = sole writer of the **Nova Echo Index** only; scar/identity effects are REQUESTS to ScarLogicCore/RIL; **Codex never**. **G5:** refuses to run under RACM/Grid/TCAML suppression. **Timer→ELIGIBILITY only** — `Mutated` is set by a **succeeded collapse attempt**, never by clock. **Scope: Engine v1, single-echo**; NSC/fusion/NDR **declared-dormant**. *(Ruled 2026-07-21; **STAGE 1 CLOSED 2026-07-22 — 110 green, `95562cf`. STAGE 2 GATED on the echo-consumption ruling**; §8.)* |
| **13** | **A Nova echo is SPENT when it authors** | **One echo backs one proposal, EVER.** A MUTATED echo that has authored is consumed — it may never back another. Record consumption as a **FIELD** on the echo (`spent_on` = the proposal id, + timestamp): it is **NOT a fifth `FermentationStatus`** — that enum is canon-closed at DORMANT/ACTIVE/DECAYING/MUTATED and **stays closed** (Ruling 7's shape; `status` remains MUTATED). **`proposal_provenance` is APPEND-ONLY** — writing a key that already exists must **RAISE**, never overwrite: an overwrite means the one-proposal-ever gate already failed, and a forensic record is never overwritten (Ruling 11). **Do NOT over-narrow:** consumption is per-**ECHO**, not per-doctrine — a second, distinct MUTATED echo may still propose for the same strained doctrine. *(Ruled 2026-07-22; **CLOSED 2026-07-23 — invariants 23/23, suite 117, content in `2b05da6`**; §8.)* |
| **14** | **`echo_origin` means a SURVIVED echo, not an existing one** | CMTE criterion 2 is an OR (`not doctrine.scar_links and not context.get("echo_origin")`), so **`echo_origin` is load-bearing ONLY for a scar-less doctrine** — it decides whether a belief with no visible fracture may evolve. **RULED: true for doctrine d iff a PROPOSAL for d exists AND the echo recorded as authoring it is MUTATED and scar-linked.** Not "an echo bears on d" (a doctrine-strain echo of a scar-less doctrine is scar-less by construction — it could never author a proposal, yet would grant eligibility for one); not "any MUTATED scar-linked echo" (Ruling 13 means it may already be SPENT on another doctrine). **CHECK G2's guarantee, don't assume it** — a proposal whose backing echo is not scar-linked is a G2 BREACH and must surface legibly. **Source-agnostic:** status + scar linkage qualify an echo, never which eruption kind made it. **Consequences, both correct:** while `proposals=None`, `echo_origin` is False everywhere — but it must be **DERIVED** to False, **NEVER a literal `False`** (that is Docket C's shape with the opposite sign, and it stops being honest the instant proposals arrive); and the doctrine-strain source makes `echo_origin` redundant, which is right — **a scar-less doctrine's own strain is not a fracture.** *(Ruled 2026-07-23; **CLOSED 2026-07-24 — STEP 1 of Stage 2b, landed BEFORE the `proposals` wiring; `c6d8b7e`, TRUE-half pins in `tests/test_nova_stage2b.py`, FALSE-half supersession in `tests/test_nova_stage2a.py`**; §8.)* |
| **15** | **Nova's `scar_requests` stay PARKED — the parking is a RULING** | `ScarLogicCore.form_scar(…, weight: float = 0.0, …)` wants a weight Nova does not honestly hold. Wiring it as-is mints **zero-weight scars — permanent, decay-aware records of a fracture that exerts no pressure**, worse than no record because they dilute the substrate of identity while looking like evidence. **Nova never supplies a scar weight and never coins one.** If fusion is wired later, Nova supplies EVIDENCE (echo id, origin, doctrine, verdict, real pressure) and the store's owner computes the magnitude — **generalizing Ruling 1: the owner owns the write INCLUDING its magnitudes; a request carrying a fabricated magnitude is a disguised write.** Also UNRULED, and to be flagged in-file: the **TRIGGER**, not just the consumer — 5a:1123's `Doctrine forged / scar fused / CSA rerouted` may be ALTERNATIVES (the code's own `csa_requests` fires on FAILURE, so the list plausibly spans outcomes), making `fuse_scar`-on-every-success an unruled reading. **Do not change that behavior — label it.** `scar_requests` stays an append-only legible accumulation: real forensics, zero effect. *(Ruled 2026-07-24; **CLOSED 2026-07-24 — the in-file UNRULED-trigger note is landed at the `scar_requests` append in `record_collapse_result`.**)* |

| **16–19** | *(Recorded in the manifest and in prose here, not as table rows: 16 closed as a REFUSAL; **17** governs test construction — §4; **18** a ⊗-fossilized id cannot be re-committed; **19** fossil revival is Option B — a NEW id carrying the fallen id in `mutation_lineage`, never the old name. Ruling 18's guard is PERMANENT, and Ruling 19's leftovers are landed: `codex.py`'s comment reads RULED, and `tests/test_codex.py` pins the new-id-with-fossil-lineage birth.)* |
| **20** | **Origin-matched echo selection** | An echo may back a proposal for doctrine D **only if `origin_kind == "doctrine_strain"` AND `origin_id == D`.** `proposals()` matched by SORT ORDER — `qualifying.pop(0)` off a globally id-sorted list while iterating `sorted(fragments)`, **two orderings with no relationship** — so with two doctrines strained at once the pairing silently crossed. **The damage was not misattribution but LINEAGE CONTAMINATION:** the emitted proposal merges the echo's `scar_links`, and DEE hands `scar_links[0]` to SAE as collapse lineage — a mispaired echo writes another doctrine's scars into the successor's permanent lineage. Forgery by accident, through a seam neither G2 nor G3 watches. No match → the EXISTING refusal path (ferment, record); **NEVER a substitute.** The other four origin kinds name a scar/verdict/fragment, not a doctrine — **excluded LEGIBLY, not silently skipped.** Ties → oldest id, deterministic. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **21** | **Backing-echo lineage** | CMTE criterion 2 is an OR (scar **or** echo origin), but `_approve` read only the first half, so a scar-less doctrine admitted on `echo_origin` got `""` and SAE refused it — **Ruling 14's positive half was DEAD CODE.** Resolution order: the doctrine's own scar → else the proposal's `scar_links[0]` (which `proposals()` already populates from the backing echo) → else `""`. **`""` is STILL REFUSED by SAE — that guard is correct and stays.** Nothing coined; the data already existed. This is not a widening of AVT.017 — it is AVT.017 finally being **satisfiable by the second of the two sources CMTE always named.** *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **22** | **Scar snapshot-on-read** | `get_active_scars()` / `get_scar()` return **DEEP COPIES**, mirroring `Codex._snapshot()`. The doctrine store had an ownership **BOUNDARY**; the scar store had a **CONVENTION** — a caller could set `.weight`, clear `.linked_doctrines`, or flip `.decay_state` with no owner-controlled operation, and **the AST single-writer invariant CANNOT see it** (nothing assigns to `scar_core.scars`). Scars are the most permanent records in the system, and a permanence enforced only by everyone remembering not to touch it is not permanence. **`_find()` is the owner's private live lookup** — `decay_scar` resolves through it, NOT through the public accessor; routing an owner write through a snapshotting read makes it vanish **silently**, the worst outcome of this change. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **23** | **DMW overflow is a REFUSAL and is recorded as one** | The 32-slot cap is **CORRECT and does not move** — bounded queues are how this system refuses to become an overload vector. **The SILENCE was the defect:** past the cap a new strained doctrine hit a bare `continue`, while twenty lines above the expiry path routed through `_ferment()` with a reason string — same file, one exit legible, the other silent. Overflow now lands on `dmw.last_overflow` and DEE routes it to **the same durable surface as every other DMW outcome** (recorded ruling + Veiled Thread + CAE), carrying the doctrine id, its real strain magnitude, and "queue at capacity". **Unresolved pressure never leaves silently.** No magnitude coined. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **24** | **Mutation is made atomic by PRE-FLIGHT, never by rollback** | `SAE.mutate_doctrine` runs three checks **before any write** (and before `authorize()`, so a refusal does not also spend a ceiling slot): (i) `new_form.id != doctrine_id`; (ii) not in `fossils`; (iii) not in `doctrines` — **(iii) would have SILENTLY CLOBBERED a live doctrine**, an unexamined third defect found while ruling this one. Same-id mutation was **ALWAYS broken**: pre-Ruling-18 it produced dual presence (active AND fossil, durable across reload); post-Ruling-18 it fossilizes the ancestor and then raises, **vanishing the doctrine**. **ROLLBACK IS REFUSED OUTRIGHT** — an "un-fossilize" moves an id from `fossils` back to `doctrines`, mechanically the revival Rulings 18/19 forbid; building it "for rollback only" creates an executable bypass of both. **The wrong path must be UNEXECUTABLE, not undoable.** Ruling 18's guard is untouched and never fires on the legitimate path. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **25** | **A structural violation is not an error message** | `process_input` catches AUREA's OWN guards in a clause ordered **BEFORE** the broad one. One `except Exception` had flattened `CodexWriteViolation`, `CeilingExceeded`, `UngatedReflexViolation`, `UngroundedEchoViolation`, `ProvenanceOverwriteViolation` and every other deliberate raise into the same string as a malformed-input hiccup — **the entire "raise, don't resolve" discipline terminating in a string concatenation. A guard whose firing looks like a typo is not enforcement.** On fire: a **distinct loud field** (`result['structural_violation']`, never merged into `errors`), **normal output SUPPRESSED** (she does not answer as though nothing happened when her own guard fired), and a **durable record** (`structural_violations` + `logs/structural_violations.jsonl`, best-effort, never raising). It does **NOT crash the process** — that would destroy the record, and the record is the point. `STRUCTURAL_VIOLATIONS` is a **CLOSED, ENUMERATED tuple of concrete types — never a base class** that might catch something unintended; a new guard joins it deliberately. **Ordinary exceptions still degrade into `errors` — the taxonomy CUTS, it does not replace.** *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **26** | **Bidirectional scar↔doctrine reads** | Any code resolving "does scar S bear on doctrine D" checks BOTH `doctrine.scar_links` and `scar.linked_doctrines` — a UNION of two partial views, not either alone. `aurea_core:850`'s pressure path already read both; eruption read one, so a doctrine referenced only via `scar.linked_doctrines` got pressure but produced a SCARLESS echo that could never author (failing CMTE criterion 2 both ways — exactly the case Rulings 14/21 exist for). **Unblocks Ruling 21's positive half through the wired eruption source.** Underlying duplicate state (two stores, two halves, no derivation) parked as hygiene — bidirectional reads make it correctness-neutral, not the eventual fix. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **CLOSED 2026-07-25** — §8.)* |
| **27** | **TCAML GLOBAL lock — Arbitration Protocol v2.0 made structural, model-checked** | Base protocol (LOCAL: no check; GLOBAL: synchronous `lock_request` → grant/deny, never cached; meta-instability auto-deny) was already drafted in the corpus and is UNCHANGED. Three additions: **bounded hold** (`TTL=5`, canon reuse — but MUST have scheduling priority over other GLOBAL housekeeping once due, or the bound is empirical, not exact — model-checked, see `Aurea/docs/formal/tcaml_lock/`); **meta-instability onset REVOKES a held lock**, not just future requests (model-checked NECESSARY — the weaker reading fails a safety property in 2 steps, concrete counterexample in the README); **Docket F's structural-topology measures select a threshold tier, never deny directly**. Three decision points flagged for architect confirmation, not silently closed — see manifest Ruling 27. *(Ruled 2026-07-25; **FULLY CLOSED 2026-07-26. Stage 1 (organ) `00e258a`+`1d2a3e6`; Stage 2 (wiring) — TCAML is LIVE in the pipeline, `RACM._request_lock()`'s build-stage default-grant branch is DELETED, and CSE's realignment requests reach a real owner. Suite 251, invariants 25/25.** — §8.)* |
| **28** | **Betweenness REPORTS, it never ELEVATES** | The four elevating conditions are each a **discrete structural fact** — an articulation point is removed or it isn't, a bridge is severed or it isn't — checkable with **no magnitude**. Betweenness is **CONTINUOUS**, so any trigger built on it needs a cutoff, and that cutoff would be a coined magnitude at the organ's most safety-critical decision. **§9 standing bar #5, refused a THIRD time.** The ARGMAX formulation is endorsed as the better refusal — it needs no threshold at all. `betweenness_hubs_removed` is **DIAGNOSTIC/FORENSIC ONLY**; the `_UNRULED` suffix is dropped because a field name asserting an open question that is closed is a stale status line. **REOPENING CONDITION (neither half optional): a cutoff RECOVERED from corpus, or demonstrated by operational correlation — NEVER an invented one.** The float tie-tolerance stays: a NUMERIC TOLERANCE IS NOT A DECISION THRESHOLD. *(Ruled 2026-07-26; **CLOSED 2026-07-26** — §8.)* |
| **29** | **A revoked holder's release is a DIFFERENT EVENT from a never-holder's** | Keeping the raise was CORRECT — swallowing it makes a revoked operation indistinguishable from a completed one. But one type covering both causes is **Ruling 25's defect one level down**. `LockReleaseViolation` = the caller **NEVER HELD** it (caller error, upstream gate failed). `StaleLockRelease` = TCAML **TOOK** it (revoked by instability onset, or force-expired at TTL — system action, **caller blameless**), and the message names WHICH. Both join `STRUCTURAL_VIOLATIONS`; **neither is a base class of the other.** Matching is on the **(action_id, module_id) PAIR** — a different module releasing someone else's revoked lock never held it either, and widening that match would let real ownership confusion hide behind the blameless type. **`StaleLockRelease` IS the abandoned-state marker for the current era ONLY** — when a persistence contract lands it becomes the TRIGGER for a real resumable state, not the whole answer. *(Ruled 2026-07-26; **CLOSED 2026-07-26** — §8.)* |
| **30** | **The GLOBAL lock guards STRUCTURAL CHANGE, not breadth** | Resolves the GSR/Rule-3 conflict — **NEITHER side bends.** Two senses of "scope" were conflated at `racm.py:590` (`claim.scope.value` → `lock_request`): Ruling 11's **REFLEX durability scope** (GSR GLOBAL → immediate flush — UNTOUCHED) vs the Arbitration Protocol's **ACTION lock scope**, whose corpus GLOBAL list (expansion, mutation, registration, topology change, doctrine-spine write) is exhaustively STRUCTURAL and contains no suppressive verb. Breadth does not change an action's class — a system-wide suppression cascade is restrictive at maximum breadth, still not mutation. **The lock stops the system CHANGING itself while unstable, not PROTECTING itself.** Lock requests key on the action's STRUCTURAL CLASS; today no reflex action is structural, so no reflex requests the lock — which RESTORES the Quint model's own clientele (`ACTORS = {mspInstall, doctrineRemap}`; reflexes were never modeled as lock clients). No actor exemption enters Rule 3 (`noGrantDuringInstability` stays model-checked); RACM's coined GSR arbitration exemption survives. *(Ruled 2026-07-26; **CLOSED 2026-07-26 — `LockClass{STRUCTURAL,NON_STRUCTURAL}` in `tcaml.py`, TYPE-ENFORCED at `lock_request`; suite 256, invariants 25/25.** — §8.)* |
| **31** | **A forensic write path that cannot be REDIRECTED is one that WILL be polluted** | **Path injectability is part of a durable store's CONTRACT, not a testing convenience.** `GSR._default_alert` wrote its log from a BARE LITERAL in a method body. Every other durable store resolves its path from a class attribute (`RBSystem.DEFAULT_LOG_PATH`, `AureaCore.STRUCTURAL_LOG_PATH`) or an `__init__` default (CSA · VeiledThread · BlackSphere) — **exactly and only the two shapes `conftest.py` can monkeypatch.** A literal is neither, so the path was **UNREACHABLE by the isolation fixture, not merely uncovered**, and every GSR-driving run appended to the real forensic log (10 stale entries, one from 2025-08-10). **An isolation fixture covering four of five paths does not provide isolation — it provides the APPEARANCE of it, and the appearance is what stops anyone looking**: Ruling 22's fail-silent shape relocated into the harness. Now `GSR.GSR_ALERT_PATH`, resolved at WRITE time. **A `.gitignore` line alone was INERT — both logs were TRACKED**; they are untracked (`git rm --cached`, history never amended, so nothing is destroyed) and both globs ignored. *(Ruled 2026-07-26; **CLOSED 2026-07-26 — suite 262, invariants 25/25.**)* |
| **32** | **The SEED is READ-ONLY INPUT — runtime state never writes to a tracked path** | `Codex`, `ScarLogicCore` and `EchoMemory` each had ONE `filepath` doing two incompatible jobs: the SEED read at construction, and the target of `save_to_file` — **which writes mode `"w"`.** A default-constructed store that saved therefore REPLACED Doctrine-0.1 *"Fracture Carried"* and scar Δ17 wholesale. **This is not Ruling 31's hazard:** R31 closed APPEND-mode logs where pollution ADDS junk; this is a WHOLE-FILE OVERWRITE of an identity store — not false pressure, **IDENTITY REPLACEMENT.** Nothing prevented it but that no test happened to do it: CONVENTION, against a bar of UNEXECUTABILITY. **Copy-on-first-run was REJECTED** — it leaves the seed live in the write path behind a conditional; the split leaves it **WITH NO WRITER.** **MINIMAL SEMANTICS, nothing richer without a new ruling:** load → runtime if present ELSE seed; save → always a full snapshot to runtime. **Seeds STAY TRACKED** — untracking them is the opposite remedy. Runtime under `data/runtime/`, gitignored. *(Ruled 2026-07-26; **CLOSED 2026-07-26 — suite 278, invariants 25/25.**)* |

| **33** | **The HAIL grounding contract — the ORE→HAIL boundary is a typed, FROZEN `TruthPacket`, and the render layer can structurally reach NOTHING ORE did not put in it** | The packet carries BOTH vocabularies as separately-typed fields: `collapse_verdict` (`echonet.Verdict` — TRUTH CONTENT, reused never redeclared) and `expression_verdict` (new plain non-`str` `ExpressionVerdict{SPEAK, SOFTENED, WITHHOLD, SUSPEND, FRAGMENT, REDIRECT}` — the RENDER INSTRUCTION). Member names DISJOINT (SUSPEND ≠ SUSPENDED), **pinned not assumed**. One-way authority made unexecutable THREE ways: packet FROZEN; **HAIL holds NO references** (no `__init__`, `render` is a `@staticmethod`, AST-pinned to import no store); and **verdict dispatch PRECEDES mode** — the WITHHOLD/SUSPEND branches hand off to `_render_silent(expression)`, whose **parameter list contains no packet, no mode, no content**, so a mode cannot make a withheld truth speak *by scope, not by discipline*. `tone_weight` **REPORTS, never GATES** (§9 bar #5, fourth application). Five canon modes DECLARED, three implemented; BRIDGE/MIRROR refuse legibly on a NAMED unmet precondition. **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-07-26, manifest fifth addendum; closes Docket G. **FULLY CLOSED 2026-07-26. Stage 1 (organ) `22a1207`; Stage 2 (wiring) `8482003` — HAIL is LIVE: all ten `process_input` exits emit through `ORE.resolve_path() → HAIL.render()`, `result['output_blocked']` is READ FROM THE PATH CONTRACT (one source of truth, AST-pinned to a single assignment site), and PSI's directive reaches a real consumer. Nine-scenario dump: ZERO blocked↔unblocked flips. Suite 340, invariants 25/25.** — §8.)* |

| **34** | **RESTART IS NOT ABSOLUTION — the epoch is a CONTINUITY OBLIGATION, not a session counter** | `SAE.__init__` built `epoch=0, epoch_count=0, touched_lineages=set()` and SAE had **NO save and NO load**, so the Self-Mutation Ceiling reset on process death: **3 mutations per epoch became 3N across N restarts.** Worse, `stabilization_event` — the ONLY legitimate closer — **has no caller anywhere in `src/`**, so the legitimate reset was unwired and the illegitimate one worked. **A guard pointed the wrong way.** The question was never *should `epoch_count` persist*: **`epoch_count` is DOWNSTREAM of the settle condition**, and what a resumed AUREA must answer is not *what did I have* but **what did I still owe**. **(1) STATE IS DURABLE** under `data/runtime/` (Ruling 32's prefix), Ruling 32's minimal semantics **VERBATIM** — whole-file snapshot, no layering, no delta, no merge rule — and **THERE IS NO SEED EPOCH**: a missing file is a first run, because an epoch is ACCUMULATED, never issued. Durable **at the moment of spending**, not at the next boundary (a bare `authorize()` in a process that dies mid-pass would otherwise restore the slot — found by mutation, not by design). **(2) CLOSURE DISCHARGES WHAT SETTLED, NEVER ERASES WHAT DID NOT** — `touched_lineages.clear()` is now a CARRY. *One principle, two boundaries.* **(3) `anchor_consolidation` GETS THE SYMMETRICAL EVIDENCE GUARD** — it closed unconditionally while `scar_fermentation` was tight; the guard is hoisted so both branches require a lineage SAE actually touched. **(4) A RESTART IS RECORDED, NEVER A CLOSURE** — forensic, append-only; **no `duration` is derived** (an epoch is explicitly NOT wall-clock time, 5a:1572). **(7) `save_state`'s PATH OFF A METHOD-PARAMETER DEFAULT** → `AureaCore.STATE_PATH`, under `data/runtime/` — **Ruling 31's unreachable-by-construction defect in a THIRD shape**, now swept by an AST pin. **(8) `system_status` DROPPED from the state file** — a rendered report string written and never read back, Docket L's shape in its worst location. **Res. 5 (Nova `proposal_provenance`) and 6 (`RACM._queue`) are NOT in this pass.** *(Ruled 2026-07-27; **CLOSED 2026-07-27 — suite 422, invariants 25/25.** — §8.)* |
| **34-A** | **THE SATURATED EPOCH IS SURFACED, NEVER FORCE-CLOSED** | Persisting a counter nothing can legitimately reset LOOKS like it converts the ceiling from CONTINUOUS to PERMANENT. **Canon had already adjudicated that thirteen months earlier, in the opposite direction:** 5a:1584's anti-deadlock rule surfaces a saturated epoch "rather than force-closing the epoch, which would re-arm mutation capacity at the exact moment nothing has been metabolized." **A ceiling that stays closed while nothing settles is the INTENDED state.** The real hazard is the lock being durable **AND INVISIBLE** — Ruling 22's fail-silent shape applied to her own growth. So: `SATURATION_HORIZON = 5`, **RECOVERED NOT COINED** (5a:1587 names "the corpus's standard 5-cycle horizon"; sixth reuse of the canon magnitude), and the comparison is **STRICT** — canon says *more than* 5, so it fires on the SIXTH. **ONE SYMBOLIC CYCLE = ONE `process_input` PASS**, driven from the same site as `tcaml.tick()`; ≥1 blocked attempt increments (once per CYCLE, not per attempt), an executed mutation resets (and takes PRECEDENCE), **a cycle that did not exercise SAE changes nothing** (judgment call, pinned so it is visible). | **35** | **THE LOADER ROUTES BY STATUS — a fallen doctrine loads as a FOSSIL, and Ruling 18's guard must hold against the REAL SEED** | The tracked seed is a **FLAT LIST of eight** carrying `⊗ Doctrine-0` (status `fallen`) and `Doctrine-0` (status `locked`). `load_from_file`'s flat branch read `active, fossils = data, []`, so **the founding fossil loaded LIVE and `fossils` loaded EMPTY** — in production and in every test. **THE LAYER THAT MAKES IT A RULING:** `commit()`'s Ruling-18 guard asks `if doctrine.id in self.fossils`, so against an empty fossil map **it could never fire — a commit over `⊗ Doctrine-0` did NOT raise.** Ruling 18 was **structurally VACUOUS against the actual seed**: it protected doctrines felled at runtime and never once protected the founding fossil it was written about. **WHY NO TEST CAUGHT IT:** every test loads the seed through the same loader, so the misrouting WAS the fixture's baseline — Ruling 32's lesson in a third position, **a defect in the load path is invisible to every test that loads.** **FIX:** flat branch routes by status (`fallen` → fossils, everything else → doctrines; nested branch untouched); DRPAS iterates **`codex.active()`**, the store's own status-filtered surface, so a fallen doctrine can no longer be nominated for evolution and a **locked** one is excluded from mutation scanning while staying LIVE and READABLE via `get()`. **NO seed migration** — the seed is read-only input (Ruling 32) and stays byte-identical; the whole fix is in how it is READ. **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-07-27; **CLOSED 2026-07-27 — suite 433, invariants 26/26.** — §8.)* |

| **61** | **A PREDICTION THAT WAS NOT COMMITTED BEFORE ITS OUTCOME IS NOT A PREDICTION** | Docket O item O3. **THE PRINCIPLE:** without prior commitment a prediction is REWRITABLE until everything appears successful, so the ledger's entire job is to make the commitment UNREWRITABLE and the scoring MECHANICAL against criteria that already existed. **(1) THE COMMITMENT** — `src/external/prediction_ledger.py`: frozen `PredictionCommitment` carrying `expected_result`, `applicable_conditions`, `resolution_horizon`, the THREE criteria (success / failure / unresolved) **all fixed at commit time**, `dependency_chain`, and `claim_refs` (recorded ids only — NOT validated against the ancestry ledger, which would make this module read a second store). Deep-frozen per Ruling 52. **THERE IS NO `update`, NO `amend`, NO `revise`, AND THE ABSENCE IS PINNED AS SHAPE** — a method named `amend` with a docstring saying "only before resolution" would be a request for restraint, and this project has hard evidence restraint fails. Any criterion may be DECLARED_NONE or ABSENT: **a predictor who declared no failure criterion is on record as having declared none**, which is a different fact from never having been asked. **(2) `DependencyLink` — CLOSED, and every member is RECOVERED FROM L2'S OWN SENTENCE** (observation · causal link · auxiliary assumption · horizon · domain validity · the claim itself). Recorded AT COMMIT TIME because **a chain reconstructed after a failure is a chain drawn to explain the failure.** Recording only — this module routes nothing. **(3) THE RESOLUTION IS A SEPARATE APPEND, NEVER A MUTATION — the structural heart.** `resolve()` appends a NEW line; the commitment line is never rewritten, so the ledger reads as a HISTORY (what was expected, then what was recorded) rather than a STATE (what we now say we expected). `PredictionOutcome` closed at CONFIRMED / FALSIFIED / UNRESOLVED. **Three refusals enforced AT THE WRITE**: an unknown id, a criterion not RECORDED on that commitment, and a second resolution. **The outcome is deliberately NOT constrained to a matching criterion** — a prediction declaring only a success criterion is falsified by failing THAT criterion, and forcing a `failure_criteria` it never declared would make the honest record unwritable. **(4) THE LEDGER** — CAE's and O1's shape verbatim: append-only JSONL (mode `"a"` is the only write mode in the file, AST-pinned), Ruling 31 injectable, R3-exempt, per-line floor semantics, `PRD-{n:04d}` with **Ruling 53's sentinel WHOLE**, typed `PredictionLedgerUnreadable` in `STRUCTURAL_VIOLATIONS`, NOT in `STORE_OWNERS`. **`commit()` RAISES on write failure and the prediction DOES NOT EXIST** — O1's gate on O3's own reason: an unrecorded prediction is precisely the rewritable thing this docket abolishes. **(5) HORIZON IS A RECORDED DECLARATION, NOT A SCHEDULER.** No clock, no timer, NO auto-resolution — that would be the ledger scoring its own predictions. **The module does not interpret a horizon and cannot honestly** (it may be a date, a cycle count, or an observed condition — picking a format would COIN one at the exact point "has this expired" gets decided), so `overdue()` takes the CALLER'S judgement and supplies the record. Computed AT READ, **never stored** (L3 + Ruling 42's cached-status lesson); an overdue commitment stays UNRESOLVED and VISIBLE — the Veiled Thread's discipline applied to predictions. **(6) NOTHING HERE PROMOTES — L2, PINNED STRUCTURALLY:** no doctrine writer, no Codex handle, no scar mint, no pressure surface in the import set, and no promotion verb defined. **A CONFIRMED prediction updates NOTHING but the record.** **(7) THE O3/O4 CUT, RULED EXPLICITLY:** O3 records that a prediction was FALSIFIED against criterion X; **O4 decides what collapses because of it** (FCT-gated). **(8) REFUSED AND STANDING:** any track record, accuracy rate or calibration score — legitimate later ONLY as derivations over recorded facts, which means derived AT READ, never stored. **DIFFERENTIAL vs `fdea7c0`, 39 claims, identity run first: ZERO MOVEMENT ON EVERY SURFACE** — nothing calls this module, so a non-zero differential would itself have been the finding. **SOAK 200/5/42: every census IDENTICAL, ZERO prediction lines** (no file created), injectable table 24 → 25, and the coverage self-audit **verified to REFUSE by name** without the entry. **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-08-01; **CLOSED 2026-08-01 — suite 853, invariants 27/27; scope: NEW `src/external/prediction_ledger.py`, `aurea_core.py` (taxonomy only), `tests/conftest.py` + `scripts/soak.py` (isolation), `tests/test_ruling61.py`; ZERO pre-existing tests changed.** — §8.)* |
| **63** | **THE PROJECTION IS COMPUTED, NEVER KEPT — and it says what KIND of knowing each part of it is** | Docket O item O5. **THE GROUNDING FINDING DECIDES RES.1:** the corpus was swept for a world-model organ and has **exactly ONE hit** — ELM (6a:868) ingests "sensor data, user behavior, world state" as an INPUT STREAM routed through CPA+SPL, and ELM is UNBUILT. World state is something AUREA **RECEIVES AND FILTERS, never something she KEEPS.** *A projection that persisted would be the first stored world-model in the architecture, invented at the layer whose whole job is to refuse exactly that. Canon's silence is not an omission to fill; it is the argument.* **(1) THE CACHE IS REFUSED OUTRIGHT** — the registration offered "compute-on-read OR staleness-DECLARED" and this takes the stronger arm: *a cached projection is a stale authority waiting for a trusting reader.* **Declaring staleness is a DISCIPLINE; having no cache makes staleness STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE.** No module-level state, no instance cache, no memoization, no `lru_cache` — AST-pinned, and a memoizing mutant must fail, which is what makes the resolution real rather than stated. **(2) IT OWNS NO STORE AND WRITES NOTHING** — not in `STORE_OWNERS`, no `open`, no path; **inputs arrive as ALREADY-READ RECORDS** (O2's shape, O2's reason: a module that opens files can be made to write one). **(3) THE COMPOSED SET IS CLOSED, AND CHECKED RATHER THAN DOCUMENTED** — the O1 ancestry records and the O3 commitments and resolutions, NOTHING ELSE; a foreign type RAISES. Doctrines, scars and topology are a **FUTURE WIDENING RULING**, because composing them stops this being a view over external epistemics and makes it **the sovereign world-model the docket refused by name.** **(4) `KnowledgeTier` CLOSED at four, recovered verbatim — and OBSERVED IS STRUCTURALLY UNPRODUCIBLE AND SAYS SO AT THE MEMBER.** AUREA observes nothing; every record here arrived by ASSERTION, not measurement. The member STAYS (a closed vocabulary missing a registered member is the enum reopening later) and **no code path may emit it, AST-pinned** — Ruling 50's SOFTENED precedent exactly. Reopening: a sensor path whose records are MEASUREMENTS. **(5) THE TIER IS DERIVED FROM RECORDED FACTS AND CARRIES ITS OWN ARGUMENT** (Ruling 45's move): a frozen `TierAnnotation` holding `tier` plus **the record and FIELD that produced it** — always a real field, never prose. **`origin_kind` UNDECLARED → `tier=None`, and that is the whole point of allowing None:** a channel that declared nothing does not become REPORTED by default, which would be **L3's fabrication class re-entering at the READ side after Ruling 58 spent an entire ruling closing it at the WRITE side.** A `None` tier is **RENDERED, never omitted** — a shorter list looks like a smaller world rather than a hidden one. **NO FIFTH MEMBER COINED.** **(6) NO NUMBERS ANYWHERE**, and **no tally field either** — counts are *permitted* but not required, and a count presented by the projection is one step from a count presented as a quality signal, at exactly the surface where that would feel natural and be false. **JUDGMENT CALL ON THE RECORD:** res.5 names INFERRED as "what AUREA's own pipeline produced from other components" without naming its producer; this composition has exactly one — **a SETTLED prediction**, which no channel asserted and which her own ledger produced by composing a commitment with a resolution. Calling it REPORTED was refused because a resolution carries no `origin_kind`, so the attribution would be invented. **DIFFERENTIAL vs `5f50264`, 39 claims, identity run first: ZERO MOVEMENT ON EVERY SURFACE.** **SOAK 200/5/42: every census IDENTICAL and the injectable table UNCHANGED at 25** — a table that grew here would itself have been the finding. **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-08-01; **CLOSED 2026-08-01 — suite 881, invariants 27/27; scope: NEW `src/external/world_state.py` + `src/utils/deep_freeze.py` (the owed hoist), `mutation_proof.py` + `claim_ancestry.py` (hoist + docstring supersession), `tests/test_ruling63.py`; TWO pre-existing consumer pins migrated.** — §8.)* |
| **64** | **A PROJECTION MAY NOT NAME ITSELF AFTER WHAT IT CANNOT SEE** | **Ruling 63's CODE WAS FAITHFUL; RULING 63's CONTRACT WAS WRONG.** The contract never said what a component's `detail` carries, and the gap produced a meaning-reversing surface — **witnessed, not argued**, by the load-bearing pin written FIRST and watched RED at `8217b9c`: `WorldStateComponent(component_id='PRD-0001', detail='The bridge will hold.', annotation=TierAnnotation(tier=INFERRED, ...))` — **a FALSIFIED prediction projecting its refuted expectation in an unlabeled slot, with a tier vouching for it.** **(1) THE RENAME:** `world_state.py` → **`record_projection.py`**, `WorldStateProjection`/`WorldStateComponent` → `RecordProjection`/`RecordComponent`. **NO SHIM, NO ALIAS** — a shim preserves the lie. `ClaimAncestryRecord` deliberately never stores claim content, so this module **structurally cannot carry propositions**, and a module named for what it cannot represent is **false documentation in the strongest position a name can occupy.** **(2) `INFERRED` JOINS `OBSERVED` AS UNPRODUCIBLE.** `resolve()` accepts a **CALLER-SUPPLIED** outcome, evaluates no evidence, tests no criterion mechanically and records no adjudication provenance: **the COMPOSITION is AUREA's, the CONTENT is the caller's, and COMPOSING IT IS NOT INFERRING IT.** **AUREA has no ADJUDICATION surface as she has no SENSOR surface.** Reopening: a resolution recording WHO adjudicated and against WHICH criterion's evidence — **O4's territory, refused in Ruling 62, so the two refusals INTERLOCK and each names the other.** **Two of four tiers unproducible is the honest census of what this architecture can currently know.** **(3) CLAIM COMPONENTS STOP IMPERSONATING CONTENT** — `claim_id`, `origin_kind` and the ancestry fields' **STATES**, each labeled as the field it is; **no `detail`.** *An asserter's name in a detail slot is a proposition-shaped hole with a person's name in it.* **(4) PREDICTION COMPONENTS CARRY OUTCOME POLARITY OR THEY DO NOT PROJECT** — `expected_result` LABELED AS AN EXPECTATION, the outcome VALUE beside it, the criterion, and both refs; unresolved carries `outcome=None` explicitly, because a missing key reads as an oversight. **(5) CONTRADICTORY RESOLUTIONS RAISE** — `setdefault` first-wins let list order adjudicate between a CONFIRMED and a FALSIFIED record of one prediction; **choosing between them is an adjudication and this module has no authority to make one**, which is the same fact that makes INFERRED unproducible. **RIDERS, each correcting a closed ruling on a confirmed external-review finding: (6)** `MINTED_ID_PATTERN` is ANCHORED with explicit non-identifier boundaries — Ruling 60 res.3 said "anchored" and the code was bare, so `prefixCLM-0001suffix` **forged a descent edge**; `\b` is insufficient because `-` is itself a non-word character. **(7)** `provided(None)` RAISES — a malformed fourth state wearing the first one's name, under which **two explicit nulls compared EQUAL and became ONE SHARED_ASSERTER.** **(8)** `overdue()` consults ONLY PROVIDED horizons — a commitment that declared none is not overdue, one never asked is not knowable, and handing either to the predicate invites a caller to invent a date. **RIDE-ALONG: the DOCKET H SUBSTRING-TO-IMPORT CONVERSION, at five occurrences, is DISCHARGED** — `test_docket_h.py` and `test_ruling58.py` converted, joining the two Ruling 63 converted. **JUDGMENT CALL ON THE RECORD:** res.2 removed INFERRED without naming a replacement; a settled prediction gets **`tier=None`** — not REPORTED, because REPORTED derives from a recorded `origin_kind` and a resolution has none, so it would invent the attribution. **DIFFERENTIAL vs `8217b9c`, 39 claims, identity run first: ZERO MOVEMENT.** **SOAK: census identical, injectable table UNCHANGED at 25.** **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-08-01; **CLOSED 2026-08-01 — suite 895, invariants 27/27; scope: `record_projection.py` (renamed + rewritten), `source_genealogy.py`, `claim_ancestry.py`, `prediction_ledger.py`, `tests/`; SIX pre-existing pins migrated.** — §8.)* |
| **65** | **THE MAP IS A DERIVATION, AND A DERIVATION IS REBUILT, NEVER RESTORED** | The persisted topology was **a stored derivation being trusted over its sources.** Startup now builds the map from the stores and **reads no map file at all.** The 2× mass, the nine one-way restart edges, the moved gravity center and the `total_edges` overcount are **NOT FIXED — the path that produces them ceases to exist.** **THE FINDING THAT DECIDES IT: there is NO runtime-only node whose referent survives a restart.** Scars, doctrines and Black Sphere paradoxes all have persisted sources; **an Echo record persists NOWHERE** (EchoMemory is canonical and unwired), so a restored echo node asserts a holding no store holds. **(1) THE READ PATH IS DELETED FROM THE CLASS, NOT UNCALLED** — Ruling 61's shape, because a loader that exists but nothing calls is a loaded gun for a later "helpful" pass. The rebuild is Ruling 57's order extended by one loop: **scars → doctrines → a NEW third loop re-placing paradoxes from the Black Sphere's persisted entries**, which is what makes the deletion safe (paradox nodes previously survived a restart ONLY via the file, so the two land together). `load_report` and `quarantined_edges` leave with the read path they served. **(2) RULING 42 SLICE 2 IS SUPERSEDED FOR THIS STORE ONLY** — the topology exits the durable-source set because **it never was a source**; the restoration contract STANDS unweakened for every actual store. **(3) `save_to_file` SURVIVES, WRITE-ONLY** — kept on the forensic-record principle (this is the surface that caught the defect class), AST-pinned to have no reader in `src/`, and **an adversarial snapshot and an absent snapshot produce identical live state — a PIN, not a promise.** **(4) ECHO NODES ARE EXCLUDED, reopening condition in-file** — dropping one is a CORRECTION, not a loss: the record it shadowed was already gone. **(5) GENESIS PLACES ONCE** — the +15.0 dies by the same mechanism, ONE placement path rather than two kept in agreement. **(6) REPLACEMENT IS MASS-CORRECT AS A PROPERTY**, at BOTH levels (space and constellation); re-placement within a boot is lawful, so the ARITHMETIC is made honest rather than the operation refused. **(7) BRIDGE IDS ARE PER-BOOT DERIVATIONS**, Ruling 42 res.4's mint semantics CONSCIOUSLY NARROWED for this store. **(8) THE REBUILD REPRODUCES THE CONSTRUCTION GRAPH EXACTLY and RULING 57'S SEAM ROW STANDS UNTOUCHED** — reverse-only seed links still form NO edge; this ruling does not repair them and **must not silently perform half of that repair, which is precisely what the restart was doing.** **THE RULING'S NAMED PROPERTY: a restarted AUREA holds the SAME relational map as a fresh one.** **DIVERGENCE ON THE RECORD — pin (h)/res.8 say "21 edges"; the construction graph is 12** (21 was the RESTARTED true-unique count = 12 + the nine reverse-only), and **res.8's own principle requires 12 — pinning 21 would assert the nine DID form.** **COINS NOTHING:** deletions plus property pins. *(Ruled 2026-08-02; | **66** | **A RECORD EITHER HOLDS WHAT WAS PRESENTED OR REFUSES IT — there is no third thing where it silently holds something else** | A repr-stringified `bytearray` is a **FABRICATED RECORD VALUE**: the record claims a string was presented when it was not. L3's fabrication class arriving at the **WRITE** side, answered by Ruling 53's law — **the ledger refuses what it cannot canonically hold. REFUSAL, NEVER COERCION.** **WITNESSED, NOT ARGUED (at `0b2072c`):** one bytearray leaf PASSED the proof gate, FOSSILIZED the ancestor, INSTALLED the successor, SPENT a ceiling slot, wrote a PERMANENT CAE entry silently stringified — and THEN raised, leaving `sae.save()`/`save_state()` raising **for the rest of the process**, so no store was checkpointed again. **(1) ONE SHARED VALIDATOR** — `src/utils/record_value.py`, PURE, never mutates its input, **closed admissible set DECLARED AS DATA**: leaves `str`/`bool`/`None`/`int`/**finite** `float`; containers `list` and **`str`-keyed** `dict`. **"OVER SERIALIZED PAYLOADS" IS LOAD-BEARING PRECISION** — `scar_lineage` is a TUPLE in memory and Ruling 52 freezes lists INTO tuples, so a validator over the LIVE object would refuse every proof AUREA can build; it runs over `as_dict()`, which is also exactly what CAE and SAE serialize. **NO COERCION PATH EXISTS — AST-pinned** (no `sanitize`/`coerce`/`to_canonical`), because the moment one exists a caller under deadline uses it. **(2) IT FIRES TWICE** — at the PROOF GATE (`validate_proof`, on `mutate_doctrine`'s FIRST line, so a refusal moves NOTHING: no fossilize, no install, no slot, no CAE entry) and at EVERY WRITER. **(3) THE ERROR CARRIES THE KEY-PATH** — *a refusal that cannot say where it refused is half a refusal*; witnessed at depth (`proof.contradiction_core.evidence_blob`). **ONE COINAGE ONLY:** `NonCanonicalRecordValue`, subclassing `TypeError` (a NARROWING of what `json.dumps` already raised); the gate re-raises as the EXISTING `InvalidMutationProof`, so the taxonomy needs no new member. **THE SWEEP OUT-FOUND ITS INSTRUCTIONS: TWELVE `default=str` sites in `src/`, not the three named**, and `allow_nan=` nowhere. **NaN/Infinity are a SEPARATE half** — `default=` never sees them, so deleting it alone would not have closed them. **COINS:** one error name and the declared leaf set. *(Ruled 2026-08-02; **CLOSED 2026-08-02 — suite 957, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **67** | **AN INSTRUMENT THAT MEASURES HER AUDITS ITS OWN FOOTPRINT** | **ONE SESSION DEMONSTRATED BOTH DIRECTIONS OF THE ARGUMENT:** the differential harness silently wrote **39 lines into shared `data/runtime/logs/claim_ancestry.jsonl`** while, in that same session, the audited soak **REFUSED the identical contamination and said so.** The asymmetry was never a decision — one instrument had been given an audit and the other had not. **RESOLUTION:** the soak's self-audit is GENERALIZED; the differential becomes a real instrument (`scripts/differential.py`) rather than a scratch file rewritten per pass; **during a measurement run writes touch ONLY the instrument's injected sandbox paths**; the audit RESULT is a **REQUIRED FIELD** of both reports (*a run without its audit is incomplete and says so*); and **audit failure FAILS THE RUN LOUDLY, never silently annotates.** `compare()` REFUSES a report whose audit is absent or failing — **a contaminated measurement compared against a clean one produces a diff that looks like a finding.** **ONE IMPLEMENTATION, TWO CALLERS:** `footprint_audit` lives in the soak and the differential IMPORTS it; a second copy would be a second definition free to drift, invisibly, because both would look right alone. **THE WALL-CLOCK NORMALIZATION IS REPORTED, NOT HIDDEN** — `Echo`/`CSA` ids are minted from `datetime.now()`, so comparison normalizes `<PREFIX>-<12+ digits>` positionally; that is a WORKAROUND for a carried defect, and **the identity run exists to prove the normalization is sufficient.** *(Ruled 2026-08-02; **CLOSED 2026-08-02 — suite 957, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **68** | **THE PERCEPTION LIFECYCLE — a claim-ancestry record records A CLAIM, and a non-claim is refused at the door it arrived at** | Verification findings (g) and (h), MERGED: the ancestry orphan and the manufactured `source` are **one boundary — where perception begins — seen twice.** **(1) THE TYPE GATE.** `raw_input` must be `str` BEFORE the mint; a non-`str` arrival is refused with **NO CLM line, no echo, no node, no verdict**, caller-visible in `OutputPath.ORDINARY_ERROR` — **EXISTING vocabulary, no new enum member, nothing coined.** An ordinary bad input is an **ORDINARY REJECTION, not a structural violation** (Docket N's form). **THE DECIDING DATUM WAS THE DEFECT'S OWN JUSTIFICATION:** the mint site's in-file comment defended its placement as keeping ledger lines *"in ONE-TO-ONE correspondence with claims actually PERCEIVED"* — the PERCEIVED semantics were not merely assumed by the docstrings and pins, they were **ASSERTED in the comment justifying the very placement that broke them.** **POSITION IS RULED:** between the suspension gate (byte-identical, untouched) and the mint — Rider R2's principle in the mint comment's own grammar: *a mind that is not running does not perceive claims, and an arrival that is not a claim is not perceived either.* **EMPTY AND WHITESPACE `str` REMAIN PERCEIVED** — the cause was always the TYPE, never emptiness. **(2) THE ONE-TO-ONE SENTENCE IS RESTORED TO UNQUALIFIED TRUTH** and pinned as behaviour: CLM lines written EQUALS echoes built. The sixty-second entry ordered the sentence to stop being used unqualified; **this ruling makes the qualification unnecessary instead — the property becomes true rather than the claim becoming careful.** **(3) THE MANUFACTURED `source` DIES AS SHAPE** — parameter, `Echo.source` field, and the `source:{source}` node tag all DELETED. **Ruling 58 DEMOTED the field and explicitly deferred the default as "not this ruling's remit"; THIS IS THE RULING IT DEFERRED TO** — demotion is discipline, and the manufacture continued underneath it (one pass carried `origin_kind=undeclared` with five ABSENT fields while reporting `source == 'user'`). Ruling 61's form: **deletion, not deprecation** — an unread legacy parameter is a loaded gun for the next caller who defaults it. Origin is reached the way Ruling 58 ruled it single: `node_id == echo.id → echo.claim_id → the ledger`. **(4) LEGACY BYTES UNTOUCHED** — forensic record, no migration, no reader-side reinterpretation. **COINS NOTHING:** one gate, one property pin, three deletions. *(Ruled 2026-08-02; **CLOSED 2026-08-02 — suite 981, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **69** | **WRITER OWNERSHIP — the mint derives from the record AT THE MOMENT OF MINTING, because a counter is a cached derivation and this house has refused that structure twice already** | Verification finding (j), the LAST. **THE FINDING THAT DECIDES IT: `_seq` WAS A CACHED DERIVATION OF THE FILE TRUSTED OVER ITS SOURCE** — derived lazily ONCE at construction, then incremented in memory forever and never re-synced. **That is the exact structure Ruling 63 refused at the projection** (*"a stale authority waiting for a trusting reader"*) **and Ruling 65 refused at the topology** (a derivation persisted as a source) — **its THIRD appearance, one layer down again, at the mint itself.** Measured at `0b2072c`: two live instances over one path, four lines, TWO distinct ids, on all three ledgers. **(1) `_seq` DIES AS STATE** — every mint derives afresh. Ruling 53's refusal semantics UNCHANGED (underived → the typed error, never a fallback number), and its transient-recovery re-derive is **SUBSUMED**: a recovered ledger resumes from its real maximum BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by a special case anyone must remember. **(2) THE SCAN READS RAW TEXT with the anchored pattern** (Ruling 64's rider form), NOT parsed records — **any id that reached disk, INCLUDING ONE ON A TORN, UNPARSEABLE LINE, is seen and never reissued.** The burnt-ordinal rule stops being a memory-state discipline and becomes a property of reading the file honestly. **The one accepted imprecision is STATED: a raw scan can over-count and BURN an ordinal; that is the conservative direction, and the opposite error REISSUES a live id into a record where nothing can ever disambiguate the two** (3a:112). **(3) A CLASS-LEVEL MUTEX KEYED BY RESOLVED PATH**, held across derive → mint → append — **keyed by the FILE, not the class**, because two classes pointing at one path must take the same lock. **(4) CROSS-PROCESS SINGLE-WRITER IS THE DECLARED CONTRACT** — one AUREA process per data root, which Ruling 42's durability model already assumed and this ruling STATES; **OS file locking is DECLARED OUT with its reopening condition NAMED** (a second writer process ever becoming a supported topology), so the exclusion is a ruling and not a gap. **(5) LEGACY DUPLICATE LINES ARE FORENSIC RECORD, untouched**; `resolve()`'s refusal stands exactly as the refutation found it. **COINS NOTHING:** one deletion of state, one scan discipline, one mutex, one declared contract. *(Ruled 2026-08-02; **CLOSED 2026-08-02 — suite 1024, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **70** | **A MODEL IS A SOURCE, NOT A SENSOR OF TRUTH — what it says is recorded as a claim, and a claim answers to collapse like any other** | Docket O item O6, THE LAST. **THE GATE FINDING IS THE RULING'S FOUNDATION, and it was checked BEFORE drafting per the O4 precedent:** BAR §1 bars learned/generative models IN THE TRUTH-CONTENT PATH — rendering verdicts, influencing arbitration, entering collapse scoring — and **O6 as registered requires NONE of that.** A model's output enters as a RECORDED CLAIM (`origin_kind=MODEL_PREDICTION`, a full O1 declaration) routed through the SAME perception pipeline and the SAME collapse machinery as any human assertion — **canon's XAIG law verbatim: external input enters the same collapse machinery, NOT a bypass.** It sits on the CLAIM side of the arbitration boundary, exactly where a human asserter sits, and Ruling 12 G3's bar point is ARBITRATION. Recording what a model said is L1's own move: **evidence of belief, never evidence of reality.** *"Sensor-tier provider" means INGRESS-LAYER provider, not truth-sensor* — outputs land in **PREDICTED**, which Ruling 63 already derived from `origin_kind` with no new machinery, and **OBSERVED/INFERRED remain structurally unproducible, untouched.** **Had O6 required a model's judgment to MEAN anything to a verdict, this would have been a second refusal in Ruling 62's shape.** **(1) ONE ADAPTER, PURE PLUMBING** (`src/external/model_provider.py`): it builds the O1 declaration — `asserted_by` = the DECLARED model identity **BYTE-IDENTICAL, never verified, never normalized, never parsed** — and routes the text through the EXISTING `process_input(raw_input, origin=...)`. **No second ingress; not one line of the pipeline is reimplemented.** **THE COLLABORATOR IS THE CALLABLE, NOT THE CORE** (judgment call, stated): given an `AureaCore` the adapter would hold the Codex, scar store and SAE and be *trusted* not to touch them; given `core.process_input` it holds a way to perceive a claim and **nothing else — enforcement by SCOPE, Ruling 33's move.** **(2) "EVERY O1 FIELD POPULATED" MEANS EVERY FIELD CARRIES AN EXPLICIT RECORDED STATE, not a value** — ABSENT is a real answer, and manufacturing a `basis` because a model plainly HAS one in some sense is L3's fabrication class, the exact defect Ruling 58 closed one docket item earlier. **THE ABSENT DEFAULT IS ALSO THE HONEST GENEALOGY ANSWER:** with descent undeclared, Ruling 60 reads two model claims as **UNKNOWN, never as two corroborating origins** — res.4's own reasoning, since two models sharing training corpora is the shared-ancestry case O2 exists to keep honest. **(3) THE MODEL NEVER INITIATES** — no network imports, no client libraries, no retry loops, nothing async, AST-pinned; querying/orchestration is MLOC's, gated by AVT.017. **(4) NO AUTO-COMMIT** — classifying "is this text a prediction" is INFERENCE (BAR §2-adjacent), so the adapter carries **NO import of the prediction ledger, pinned as ABSENCE**; the CALLER commits through Ruling 61's existing API. **(5) NO TRUST MACHINERY, made structural rather than promised** — the module holds **no state at all**, so there is no surface for a per-model score, weight or consensus tally to accumulate on. **(6) RULING 68'S DOOR IS THE ADAPTER'S DOOR** — the input contract is `str`, refused at THIS boundary before the pipeline is called (a plain `TypeError`: a violated type contract is the language's own vocabulary, and this is a caller's ordinary mistake, not one of AUREA's guards firing). **(7) THE TEXT GOES THROUGH VERBATIM** — no strip, no normalization, no cleaning: **a transformed assertion is a different assertion.** **DECLARED OUT with owners:** model querying/orchestration (MLOC + AVT.017), trust derivation from O3 track records (its own ruling), LCAE arbitration (unbuilt), any Codex/TCA write from anything model-shaped (**permanent, the docket's founding refusal**). **FLAGGED, NOT DECIDED:** an EMPTY identity string is accepted, and two such records would compare EQUAL and read as ONE SHARED ASSERTER — the identical failure mode Ruling 64 res.7 closed for `provided(None)`; refusing it is a value judgment this ruling does not delegate and the vocabulary's guard lives in `claim_ancestry.provided()`. **COINS NOTHING:** no enum member (`MODEL_PREDICTION` has been in O1's closed set since Ruling 58, included precisely so O6 would never reopen it), no threshold, no score. *(Ruled 2026-08-02; **CLOSED 2026-08-02 — suite 1079, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **71** | **THE SELECTOR IS UPSTREAM OF THE TRUTH IT FEEDS** | The reserved BAR §3-scope ruling, taken at Docket Q's threshold. **CLOSED BY DISPOSITION upon recording — no code this pass; its enforcement pins are assigned to the Q2 contract.** Extension of a bar's scope is a RULING, never an inference (the BAR §1 generative→discriminative extension is the procedural precedent), and this one EXTENDS BAR §3 to goal SELECTION and goal AUTHORIZATION. **The finding:** a chooser of research directions determines which evidence is ever gathered, which contradictions meet pressure, and which doctrines face evidence — **selection effects are epistemic effects entering by the side door**, and a stochastic selector biases what the system can come to know, invisibly, which is exactly the class §3 exists to bar. **(1) COMMITMENT SELECTION IS DETERMINISTIC** — the declared tie-key ladder governs (unmet dependency → oldest unresolved → nearest review horizon → least recently examined → stable identifier), Ruling 9's deferred-wins-ties form worked out in full, every key readable from the records. **(2) AUTHORIZATION IS DETERMINISTIC** — a rule evaluation over recorded state, never a draw. **(3) WITHIN-COMMITMENT EXPLORATION VARIATION IS NEITHER LICENSED NOR PERMANENTLY BARRED** — it takes ITS OWN RULING, and until that exists exploration is deterministic too. **(4) TRUTH PROMOTION IS NEVER STOCHASTIC** and **(5) A GOAL IS NEVER ACCEPTED MERELY BECAUSE A GENERATOR SAMPLED IT.** The burden stands where this house always puts it: **variation must demonstrate why a declared key is worse, not the reverse.** **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-08-03; **CLOSED BY DISPOSITION 2026-08-03 — enforcement pins land with Q2.**)* |
| **72** | **A GOAL THAT WAS NOT COMMITTED BEFORE ITS PURSUIT IS NOT A GOAL** | Docket Q item Q1, the docket's FIRST BUILD — Ruling 61's law generalized from prediction to INTENTION. Without prior commitment a goal is rewritable until whatever happened looks like what was wanted, so the ledger's whole job is to make the commitment UNREWRITABLE and the outcome MECHANICAL against criteria that already existed. **THE LEDGER IS SUBSTRATE, NOT MOVER: it stores DIRECTION; it moves nothing, wires to nothing, and grants no authority** — Q2 owns selection, Q3 owns motion, and **at the close of this pass NOTHING in `src/` imports it**, pinned. **(1) THE RECORDS, all frozen, all append-only, one jsonl ledger** (`src/goals/goal_ledger.py`): `GoalCommitment` with `GLC-####` minted through **Ruling 69's `ledger_mint` — the shared mint's SECOND consumer**, so file-derivation, the anchored pattern, the torn-line property and Ruling 53's sentinel all INHERIT rather than being re-argued; `kind` CLOSED {RESEARCH, VERIFICATION, REPAIR, EXTERNAL_TASK, CAPABILITY_ACQUISITION}; `level` CLOSED {ROOT, EPOCH, PROJECT, IMMEDIATE}, **STORED BUT NOT POLICED** (the differential mutation classes are arbitration-era work — storing a field is not the same as enforcing it, and conflating the two is how a store quietly becomes a governor, AST-pinned that the ledger never branches on `level`); `desired_state` verbatim; three id-only reference tuples **NEVER validated against their stores** (Ruling 61's `claim_refs` verbatim — the join is the caller's); the three criteria FIXED AT COMMIT; `provenance` CLOSED plus a declared `asserter` recorded **BYTE-IDENTICAL and never verified** (L1 at the goal layer — the founder is a NAMED ASSERTER, not an enum member). **(2) THE LAST TWO KINDS ARE STRUCTURALLY UNPRODUCIBLE (QL3)** — members exist so the vocabulary is closed NOW and the barrier lifts BY RULING; the public surface refuses them with a typed `UnproducibleGoalKind` naming QL3, and the reopening conditions are in-file (the action gateway; the hash-chain plus autogenesis unblocking). **(3) EVIDENCE AND RESOLUTION ARE SEPARATE APPENDS** — a resolution NAMES a DECLARED criterion or RAISES, a SECOND resolution RAISES, and **`PREMISE_INVALIDATED` is distinct from `FAILED`: a goal whose GROUND DISSOLVED did not fail**, and the two carry opposite lessons. **(4) STATUS IS DERIVED, NEVER STORED** (L3) — committed / evidence-bearing / resolved / superseded, computed at read; **evidence contributes only its EXISTENCE**, so permuted appends derive the same status BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by a sort anyone maintains. **(5) THE CRITERION VOCABULARY MAKES A ROOT STRUCTURALLY UNRESOLVABLE** — `STANDING` (no terminal state of this kind exists) and `SUPERSESSION_ONLY` are FIRST-CLASS enum members, and since `resolve` admits only DECLARED criteria, **a root cannot complete, cannot fail, and can only be SUPERSEDED. That property falls out of the vocabulary rather than being a rule anyone must remember.** **ROOT MUTATION IS SUPERSESSION** — a NEW commitment naming its predecessor, never an edit; the predecessor's bytes are untouched forever. **(6) `GoalAdoption` EXISTS AS A TYPE WITH NO PRODUCER PATH (QL6)** — nothing constructs one anywhere in `src/`, AST-pinned, because **adoption is AUREA's own event and her deliberation machinery does not exist; a founder-written adoption would be the laundering QL6 names.** Reopening: the Q3-era deliberation work. **(7) SEEDING AT GENESIS, IDEMPOTENT** — the two RG roots live in the TRACKED, writerless `data/goal_roots.json` (Ruling 32) and enter an EMPTY ledger only, as EXTERNAL_PROPOSAL / `founder` / ROOT / STANDING / SUPERSESSION_ONLY; a second genesis commits NOTHING (**restart is not absolution**), and there is no re-seed path. **(8) QL0 AS STRUCTURE** — the module imports NO SAE, Codex, RACM, reflex, expression or topology machinery: **a goal grants no authority, so the module that stores goals cannot reach anything a goal might wish to command** (Ruling 70's enforcement-by-scope, one docket later). **(9) NO priority, NO confidence, NO weight, NO numeric field of any kind** on any record — QL4, pinned as ABSENCE in three shapes. **COINS NOTHING.** *(Ruled 2026-08-03; **CLOSED 2026-08-03 — suite 1157, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **73** | **THE SELECTOR SELECTS AND RECORDS — it closes nothing, grants nothing, and draws nothing** | Docket Q item Q2. | **73-A** | **THE LADDER REORDERS — recency rotates, oldest-unresolved breaks the tie** | Corrective rider to Ruling 73, ruled the same day. **THE DEFECT WAS THE DRAFTING LANE'S AND THE FORK WAS NOT GENUINE.** Ruling 73 res.5 keyed rung 2 (OLDEST_UNRESOLVED) on the GLC ordinal — **UNIQUE per commitment**, so rung 2 was a TOTAL ORDER, rungs 3–5 were unreachable, and the ruling contradicted its own pins (b) and (f). | **78** | **WHAT REACHED THE RECORD REACHES THE DISK — and what must agree is written in the order that keeps it honest** | **THE SUBJECT IS CROSS-STORE DIVERGENCE, NOT DATA LOSS.** Every store passed its own integrity checks; the disagreement was BETWEEN them. **WITNESSED AT `37007b75`, NOT ARGUED:** a doctrine mutated through the real path, a fresh core constructed without `save_state` — `sae_epoch.json` durably recorded the mutation (durable at the moment of SPENDING since Ruling 34) while the Codex still held the ancestor LIVE and the successor was gone. Each site keeps its own `json.dumps`, validator, `allow_nan=False` and error discipline; **the helper RAISES and does not choose** — the gating ledgers still refuse, the best-effort forensic writers still catch (Ruling 11: the observer never gates the observed). **THE ROUTING IS UNEXECUTABLE-BY-OMISSION**: zero mode-`'a'` opens in `src/` outside the helper, by AST over `rglob`, with a control asserting the funnel's own append is PRESENT and a COUNT asserting a deleted append is not read as a routed one. **(3) THE IDENTITY FAMILY CHECKPOINTS AT THE EVENT BOUNDARY** — `Codex.commit` saves as its terminal act (mutation, birth and reversion all end there, so one save per EVENT), and `ScarLogicCore.add_scar` saves at the single write door, which covers every requester BY CONSTRUCTION. **TWO DELIBERATE ABSENCES, EACH PINNED:** `fossilize` does NOT save — it is always the mid-event half of a pair, and a save there would publish a torn mutation (ancestor fallen, successor absent) as a **well-formed file**; `seed` does NOT save — the tracked seed IS its durable record (Ruling 32), and genesis needs no writer. **`save_state` GAINS NO INTERNAL CALLERS** — position (a) refused in terms: *a checkpoint that fires only when someone remembers is the defect, not the fix.* **TCA stays exempt by Ruling 65**, cited not rediscovered. **THE `add_scar` CALLER CENSUS RAN FIRST**: exactly ONE caller in `src/`, `form_scar`, owner-side — no STOP, and it is now a standing pin where a second caller is a STOP rather than a number to edit. **(4) THE ORDERING LAW, RATIFIED AND PINNED AS ORDER:** spend persist → content save → record persist. *A crash may lose CONTENT against a durably spent budget — conservative and detectable — and may NEVER hold content against an UNSPENT budget, which is a free mutation.* No code was reordered; what was missing was the law. **PIN (i) IS NOT VACUOUS AND THE SLATE PROVES IT** — the true inversion (save moved after the record persist, content never lost) reddens pin (i) ALONE, with every durability pin still green. **THE REBORN-ID HAZARD IS WITNESSED DEAD** in both halves: durable joins resolve across restart, and the next mint sits strictly above every id any durable record carries. **DIFFERENTIAL vs `37007b75`: ZERO MOVEMENT ON EVERY SURFACE**, identity run CLEAN first — same writes, earlier durability. Soak census IDENTICAL on every substantive key; all five seeds the SAME GIT BLOBS. **FSYNC COST MEASURED AND REPORTED AS A FACT, NEVER A GATE: ~+0.6 ms per claim cycle (~3%), inside the baseline's own run-to-run spread.** **COINS: `durable_append_text`.** Nothing else — no enum, no threshold, no score. *(Ruled 2026-08-09; **CLOSED 2026-08-09 — suite 1452, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **79** | **THE DISAGREEMENT IS REPORTED IN HER OWN VOCABULARY, AND THE REPORT OBLIGATES NOTHING** | Registered by Ruling 78 res.4.iii. R78 made every write durable and ratified the ordering law, which shrank cross-store divergence to the crash instants between adjacent durable writes inside ONE event — plus whatever legacy residue already sits in a long-lived data directory. **THAT RESIDUE IS READABLE, WELL-FORMED, AND INVISIBLE TO EVERY PER-STORE CHECK, because each store IS internally consistent — the disagreement was BETWEEN them.** **(1) A PURE DERIVATION, SEPARATELY HOMED** — `src/retrieval/divergence.py`, `record_joins.py`'s sibling in shape and law: **stdlib-only, imports NO store**, owns no file, opens none, takes ALREADY-READ records and returns findings. **THAT PURITY IS WHAT MAKES "NEVER WRITES TO ANY STORE IT READS" STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN PROMISED** — a module that cannot reach a store cannot be talked into repairing one. It is also why `ledger_mint.ordinal_pattern` is NOT imported despite solving the ordinal problem exactly (that module opens files) and why its REGEX is not re-declared either (a second definition of a ruled pattern): `_ordinal` strips a caller-supplied prefix and requires the ENTIRE remainder to be digits — **stricter than the anchored scan, not weaker**, because it validates a whole recorded field rather than hunting an id inside arbitrary text. **(2) A POSITIVE OBLIGATES A REPORT AND NOTHING ELSE, ruled against both alternatives in terms.** **QUARANTINE REFUSED:** Ruling 51 quarantines what cannot be ADJUDICATED; a divergence is the opposite — both files readable, both well-formed, and the surviving prefix state is one **R78's ordering law ALREADY ADJUDICATED** as honest crash residue, so quarantining would re-litigate a closed ruling at load time. **REFUSAL REFUSED:** crash residue must never be fatal — a detector that stopped construction converts a survived crash into an unsurvivable one. **REPAIR FORBIDDEN OUTRIGHT, not deferred:** backfilling fabricates history (Rulings 58/70's class), and the prohibition is pinned by DRIVING a real divergence and asserting every read store is BYTE-UNCHANGED. **(3) THE VOCABULARY IS COINED AND CLOSED AT FOUR** — `EPOCH_COUNT_AHEAD`, `AUDIT_WITHOUT_RECORD`, `UNRESOLVED_JOIN`, `REFERENCED_ABOVE_FLOOR` — each detectable **FROM ONE SIDE WITHOUT GUESSING**, which is the property that makes the instrument honest: none requires knowing what SHOULD have been there. All four names were censused against every enum in `src/` (49 classes, 213 member names) before being coined. **`target_content_present` RIDES AS A FACT, NEVER AS A SUBCLASS** — content-present means the mutation happened and lost only its record, content-absent means it did not, and splitting them into two kinds would make the module adjudicate which world it is in. **`REFERENCED_ABOVE_FLOOR` IS THE REBORN-ID HAZARD'S AT-REST SIGNATURE** — R78 refused to fake page-cache loss and named this as the alternative; the citation and the floor simply disagree, and no crash needs simulating to read that. **ERA HONESTY IS LAW: a `None` join is ABSENT, never a finding** — legacy records yield zero findings forever, and an UNDERIVED floor (Ruling 53's sentinel) reports nothing rather than comparing against an invented zero. **NO severity, NO score, NO threshold, NO advice field** (§9 bar #5 at a new surface). **(4) THE RUN SITE.** ONCE at construction, after the loads, before the first input — after because there is nothing to compare until each store has said what it holds, before because a claim processed first would begin writing the very records being compared; **AST-pinned to `__init__` and to being its LAST act**, so a re-scan on a tick (DECLARED OUT, owned by a later ruling) cannot arrive quietly. Findings append to `data/runtime/logs/divergence.jsonl` through `durable_append_text` — **the funnel's next consumer, R78's routing pin already standing.** **ZERO FINDINGS WRITE NOTHING: silence is the healthy state**, the file exists only if a finding ever did, and liveness is proven by the four witnesses rather than by heartbeat lines that would move every census in the tree. Best-effort **NEVER-RAISE** (Ruling 11's valence — the detector observes what already happened and gates nothing), with its OWN failure list rather than a share of `structural_log_failures` (Ruling 29: one surface covering two causes cannot say which record never landed). **NOTHING IN `src/` READS THE LOG BACK INTO A DECISION — EL1's cousin, pinned tree-wide as structure.** **THE FOUR WITNESSES ARE MANUFACTURED THROUGH REAL STORES, EACH EDIT CHOSEN TO PRODUCE EXACTLY ONE KIND** — harder than it looks, because the naive edits overlap (deleting a CLM line both orphans the join AND drops the floor beneath the id citing it); where an edit would honestly fire two kinds a different edit was chosen rather than a looser assertion. **COINS:** the module name, the four kind names, the finding record's field names. Nothing else. *(Ruled 2026-08-09; **CLOSED 2026-08-09 — suite 1477, invariants 27/27.** — §8.)* |
| **80** | **DOCKET R ITEMS R4 + R5 — the corpus learns to ask what SURVIVES, and what a DOOR REFUSES** | Ruling 77 declared `restart_continuity` absent "with its owner"; **this is that owner arriving**, and it made exactly the decision the old text reserved. **ZERO `src/` MOVEMENT** — the runner grew, the corpus grew, and not one line of her changed. **(1) THE `operation` FIELD** — a case may name a SCRIPTED SEQUENCE from a CLOSED vocabulary, and the loader refuses an unknown one exactly as it refuses an unknown path or fact key (a case naming a sequence the runner lacks would otherwise fall through to the ordinary drive and report green for a law it never exercised). **The alternative was encoding the scenario in `input` and branching on it**, which would make the corpus's most load-bearing field mean two things by category — the overloaded-field defect, in the schema this docket exists to keep honest. **(2) THE RESTART OPERATIONS RECONSTRUCT `AureaCore` AND OBSERVE THE RESUMED ONE**, which is what makes their facts answer *did this survive the boundary* rather than *did this happen*. **NO `save_state` IS CALLED ANYWHERE** — Ruling 78 made the durable writes eager precisely so a restart needs no cooperation, and calling the checkpoint would test the checkpoint instead of the law. **(3) FOUR NEW FACT KEYS**, each derived from a real read surface as the original eight are: `doctrine_present` (R78 pin (d)'s law), `mint_above_floor` (R69+R78's floor law at rest), `door_refused` — **EL2's key, the one that says a REFUSAL IS A SUCCESS STATE** — and `stop_condition` (R74/QL5: *it closed* is a weaker claim than *it closed FOR THIS REASON*). **NO DISPOSITION VOCABULARY IS COINED;
